calcWeight(shape, dims, density) {\n let vol = 0, formula = '';\n if(shape==='plate') {\n vol = dims.L * dims.W * dims.T;\n formula = dims.L+' \u00d7 '+dims.W+' \u00d7 '+dims.T+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n } else if(shape==='bar_rnd') {\n if(dims.ID && dims.ID > 0) {\n vol = Math.PI/4 * (dims.D**2 - dims.ID**2) * dims.L;\n formula = '\u03c0/4 \u00d7 (\u00d8'+dims.D+'\u00b2 - \u00d8'+dims.ID+'\u00b2) \u00d7 '+dims.L+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n } else {\n vol = Math.PI/4 * dims.D**2 * dims.L;\n formula = '\u03c0/4 \u00d7 \u00d8'+dims.D+'\u00b2 \u00d7 '+dims.L+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n }\n } else if(shape==='bar_sq') {\n vol = dims.A**2 * dims.L;\n formula = dims.A+'\u00b2 \u00d7 '+dims.L+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n } else if(shape==='bar_hex') {\n vol = (Math.sqrt(3)/2) * dims.S**2 * dims.L;\n formula = '(\u221a3/2) \u00d7 '+dims.S+'\u00b2 \u00d7 '+dims.L+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n } else if(shape==='pipe') {\n vol = Math.PI/4 * (dims.OD**2 - dims.ID**2) * dims.L;\n formula = '\u03c0/4 \u00d7 (OD'+dims.OD+'\u00b2 - ID'+dims.ID+'\u00b2) \u00d7 '+dims.L+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n }\n return {kg: vol * density / 1e6, formula};\n}\n\n\nfunction updateProdPipeHint() {\n const si = parseInt(document.getElementById('dim-PP_SPEC')?.value||'0');\n const tp = document.getElementById('dim-PP_TYPE')?.value || 'fin';\n const hint = document.getElementById('prod-pipe-hint');\n if(!hint) return;\n const p = STKM_PRICES[si];\n const keyMap = yi_raw:'\u76ca\u967d', dy_raw:'\u6771\u7950', yi_plt:'\u76ca\u967d', jy_plt:'\u91d1\u4e9e\u6d32'};\n const keys = keyMap[tp];\n const cands = keys.map(k=>({name:nameMap[k], px:p[k]})).filter(x=>x.px);\n if(!cands.length) {\n hint.innerHTML = '
\u26a0 \u6b64\u898f\u683c\u7121\u5831\u50f9\uff0c\u8acb\u8a62\u554f\u5ee0\u5546
';\n } else {\n const best = cands.reduce((a,b)=>a.px>b.px?a:b);\n hint.innerHTML = '
\u6700\u9ad8\uff1a'+best.name+' '+best.px+' \u5143/mm\u3000'+cands.map(x=>x.name+':'+x.px).join('\u3000')+'
';\n }\n}\n\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// \u4e3b\u8a08\u7b97\n// return; }\n\n const matVal = document.getElementById('mat').value.split('|');\n const matName = matVal[0];\n let density = parseFloat(matVal[1]);\n const g = id => parseFloat(document.getElementById(id)?.value) || 0;\n\n let specDesc = '', impliedPx = 0, dbPrices = [], sawFee = 0, unit = '\u5143/kg', weight = 0;\n\n // \u2500\u2500 \u6210\u54c1\u7ba1 \u2500\u2500\n if(currentShape==='prod_pipe') {\n const si = parseInt(document.getElementById('dim-PP_SPEC')?.value||'0');\n const tp = document.getElementById('dim-PP_TYPE')?.value || 'fin';\n const L = g('dim-L');\n if(!L) { alert('\u8acb\u586b\u5165\u9577\u5ea6'); return; }\n const p = STKM_PRICES[si];\n const typeLabel = {fin:'\u6210\u54c1\u7ba1',raw:'\u7d20\u7ba1',plt:'\u96fb\u934d\u7ba1'};\n const keyMap = {\n fin:[{name:'\u76ca\u967d',k:'yi_fin'},{name:'\u6771\u7950',k:'dy_fin'},{name:'\u91d1\u4e9e\u6d32',k:'jy_fin'}],\n raw:[{name:'\u76ca\u967d',k:'yi_raw'},{name:'\u6771\u7950',k:'dy_raw'}],\n plt:[{name:'\u76ca\u967d',k:'yi_plt'},{name:'\u91d1\u4e9e\u6d32',k:'jy_plt'}]\n };\n dbPrices = keyMap[tp].map(x=>({name:x.name,px:p[x.k]})).filter(x=>x.px);\n impliedPx = purchaseAmt / L;\n unit = '\u5143/mm';\n specDesc = 'STKM13C '+typeLabel[tp]+' \u00d8'+p.id+'\u00d7\u00d8'+p.od+' \u00d7 '+L+' mm';\n }\n // \u2500\u2500 \u786c\u927b\u8ef8\u5fc3 \u2500\u2500\n else if(currentShape==='shaft') {\n const idx = parseInt(document.getElementById('dim-SH_DIA').value);\n const mat = document.getElementById('dim-SH_MAT').value;\n const L = g('dim-L');\n if(!L) { alert('\u8acb\u586b\u5165\u9577\u5ea6'); (purchaseAmt - sawFee) / weight : 0;\n unit = '\u5143/kg';\n } else {\n // \u2500\u2500 SUS / AL \u5143/mm \u8a08\u50f9 \u2500\u2500\n impliedPx = (purchaseAmt - sawFee) / L;\n unit = '\u5143/mm';\n }\n const pmMap = {stkm:'STKM13A', sus:'SUS304\u7ba1', al:'\u92c1\u7ba1'};\n specDesc = pmMap[pipeMat]+' \u00d8'+ID+'\u00d7\u00d8'+OD+' \u00d7 '+L+' mm';\n }\n // \u2500\u2500 \u677f\u6750 + \u68d2\u6750 \u2500\u2500\n else {\n let dims={}, valid=true;\n if(currentShape==='plate') {\n dims={L:g('dim-L'),W:g('dim-W'),T:g('dim-T')};\n if(!dims.L||!dims.W||!dims.T) valid=false;\n } else if(currentShape==='bar_rnd') {\n const idVal=parseFloat(document.getElementById('dim-ID')?.value)||0;\n dims={D:g('dim-D'),L:g('dim-L'),...(idVal>0?{ID:idVal}:{})};\n if(!dims.D||!dims.L) valid=false;\n } else if(currentShape==='bar_sq') {\n dims={A:g('dim-A'),L:g('dim-L')};\n if(!dims.A||!dims.L) valid=false;\n } else if(currentShape==='bar_hex') {\n dims={S:g('dim-S'),L:g('dim-L')};\n if(!dims.S||!dims.L) valid=false;\n }\n if(!valid) { alert('\u8acb\u586b\u5165\u6240\u6709\u5c3a\u5bf8'); (purchaseAmt-sawFee)/weight : 0;\n\n // \u898f\u683c\u63cf\u8ff0\n const matDisp = matName.replace('S45C_L','S45C \u5149\u4e2d\u78b3').replace('S45C_B','S45C \u9ed1\u4e2d\u78b3');\n if(currentShape==='plate') specDesc=matDisp+' \u677f\u6750\u3000T'+dims.T+' \u00d7 L'+dims.L+' \u00d7 W'+dims.W+' mm';\n else if(currentShape==='bar_rnd') specDesc=matDisp+' \u5713\u68d2\u3000\u00d8'+dims.D+' \u00d7 '+dims.L+' mm';\n else if(currentShape==='bar_sq') specDesc=matDisp+' \u56db\u89d2\u68d2\u3000\u25a1'+dims.A+' \u00d7 '+dims.L+' mm';\n else if(currentShape==='bar_hex') specDesc=matDisp+' \u516d\u89d2\u68d2\u3000\u2b21'+dims.S+' \u00d7 '+dims.L+' mm';\n }\n\n // \u6bd4\u8f03\u5217\u8868\n let compRows = '';\n if(dbPrices.length>0) {\n compRows = dbPrices.map(v=>{\n if(!v.px) return '';\n const diff = (impliedPx - v.px) / v.px * 100;\n const absDiff = Math.abs(diff);\n let cls='diff-ok', mark='\u2713 \u5408\u7406';\n if(absDiff>10){cls='diff-alert'; mark=diff>0?'\u26a0 \u504f\u9ad8':'\u26a0 \u504f\u4f4e';}\n else if(absDiff>5){cls='diff-warn'; mark=diff>0?'\u25b3 \u7565\u9ad8':'\u25b3 \u7565\u4f4e';}\n const sign=diff>=0?'+':'';\n return `
\n ${v.name}\u3000${fmtPx(v.px)} ${unit}\n ${sign}${diff.toFixed(1)}%\u3000${mark}\n
`;\n }).join('');\n } else {\n compRows='
\u6b64\u898f\u683c\u7121\u5167\u5efa\u8cc7\u6599\u5eab\u884c\u60c5\uff0c\u7121\u6cd5\u6bd4\u8f03
';\n }\n\n const sawNote = sawFee>0 ? `
\u6263\u9664\u5207\u5de5\u8cbb\u2212 NT$ ${fmtNT(sawFee)}

calcWeight(shape, dims, density) {\n let vol = 0, formula = '';\n if(shape==='plate') {\n vol = dims.L * dims.W * dims.T;\n formula = dims.L+' \u00d7 '+dims.W+' \u00d7 '+dims.T+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n } else if(shape==='bar_rnd') {\n if(dims.ID && dims.ID > 0) {\n vol = Math.PI/4 * (dims.D**2 - dims.ID**2) * dims.L;\n formula = '\u03c0/4 \u00d7 (\u00d8'+dims.D+'\u00b2 - \u00d8'+dims.ID+'\u00b2) \u00d7 '+dims.L+' \u00d7 '+density+' \u00f7 1,000,000';\n } else {\n vol = Math.PI/4 * dims.D**2 * dims.L;\n formula = '\u03c0/4 \u00d7 \u00d8'+dims.D+'\u00b2 \u00d7 1,000,000';\n }\n return {kg: vol * density / 1e6, formula};\n}\n\n\nfunction updateProdPipeHint() {\n const si = parseInt(document.getElementById('dim-PP_SPEC')?.value||'0');\n const tp = ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n// 規格查詢輔助\n// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\nfunction getSpecArr(matCode, shape) {\n if(shape===\'pipe\') return [];\n ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\nfunction lookupBarPrice(matCode, shape, dim) {\n let tbl = null;\n if(shape===\'bar_rnd\') {\n if(matCode===\'SS400\'||matCode===\'ST52\'||matCode===\'STKM13A\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.SS400_rnd;\n else if(matCode===\'S45C_L\'||matCode===\'S45C_B\'||matCode===\'S50C\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.S45C_rnd;\n else if(matCode===\'S20C\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.S20C_rnd;\n else if(matCode===\'SCM440\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.SCM440_rnd;\n else if(matCode===\'SCM415\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.SCM415_rnd;\n else if(matCode===\'SNCM439\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.SS400_sq;\n else if(matCode===\'S45C_L\'||matCode===\'S45C_B\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.S45C_rnd;\n } else if(shape===\'bar_hex\') {\n if(matCode===\'SS400\') tbl=BAR_PRICES.SS400_hex;\n }\n if(!tbl) return null;\n const row = tbl.find(r => dim <= r.max);\n if(!row) return null;\n const candidates = [{name:\'合興\',px:row.heXing},{name:\'建興\',px:row.jianXing},{name:\'豪星\',px:row.haoXing}].filter(x=>x.px);\n if(!candidates.length) return null;\n return {best:candidates.reduce((a,b)=>a.px>b.px?a:b), all:candidates};\n}\n\n// 豪星切工費查詢：回傳 {base, mult, fee, note, grpKey} 或 null\nfunction lookupSawFee(matCode, dia, length) {\n let grpKey = null;\n if([\'S45C_L\',\'S45C_B\',\'S50C\',\'SCM440\',\'SCM415\'].includes(matCode)) grpKey = \'grpA\';\n else if(matCode === \'SNCM439\') grpKey = \'grpB\';\n else if(matCode === \'SKD11\') grpKey = \'grpC\';\n if(!grpKey || dia < 50) return null;\n const row = HAO_XING_SAW_FEE.find(r => dia <= r.max);\n if(!row) return null;\n const base = row[grpKey];\n let mult = 1.0, note = \'\';\n if(length <= 20) { mult = 2.0; note = \'長≤20mm ×2倍\'; }\n else if(length <= 30) { mult = 1.5; note = \'長≤30mm ×1.5倍\'; }\n else if(length <= 50) { mult = 1.2; note = \'長≤50mm ×1.2倍\'; }\n return { base, mult, fee: Math.round(base * mult * 10) / 10, note, grpKey };\n}\n\n// 合興切工費查詢：外徑分段，長度 < 50mm 才計費\nfunction lookupHeXingSawFee(dia, length, matCode, shape) {\n if(shape !== \'bar_rnd\') return null;\n if(length >= 50) return null;\n // 確認合興對此規格有報價\n const barRes = lookupBarPrice(matCode, shape, dia);\n if(!barRes) return null;\n const hasHX = barRes.best.name === \'合興\';\n if(!hasHX) return null;\n const row = HE_XING_SAW_FEE.find(r => dia >= r.min && dia <= r.max);\n if(!row) return null;\n return { fee: row.fee, dia, length };\n}\n\n// 依廠商取切工費：有切工表用表，否則預設外徑/10\nfunction getVendorSawFee(vendorName, matCode, shape, dim, length) {\n if(![\'bar_rnd\',\'bar_sq\',\'bar_hex\'].includes(shape)) return 0;\n if(vendorName === \'合興\' && shape === \'bar_rnd\' && length < 50) {\n const row = HE_XING_SAW_FEE.find(r => dim >= r.min && dim <= r.max);\n if(row) return row.fee;\n }\n if(vendorName === \'豪星\' && shape === \'bar_rnd\') {\n const sfRes = lookupSawFee(matCode, dim, length);\n if(sfRes) return sfRes.fee;\n }\n return Math.round(dim / 10 * 10) / 10;\n}\n\nfunction lookupPlatePrice(thickness, matCode, plateType) {\n const mc = matCode || \'SS400\';\n const pt = plateType || \'square\';\n const tol = 0.01;\n const hasFuk = PLATE_SPEC_FUKGEN.some(v => Math.abs(v - thickness) <= tol);\n const hasGuan = PLATE_SPEC_GUAN.some(v => Math.abs(v - thickness) <= tol);\n\n // 特殊鋼板：顯示參考單價，提示須詢問供應商確認規格\n if(mc===\'S45C_B\'||mc===\'S45C_L\'||mc===\'S45C\') {\n return {inquiry:true};\n }\n if(mc===\'S50C\') {\n const cands=[{name:\'高碳\',px:37},{name:\'梧濟\',px:38}];\n return {best:cands.reduce((a,b)=>a.px>b.px?a:b), all:cands, confirm:true};\n }\n if(mc===\'SCM440\') {\n const cands=[{name:\'高碳\',px:59}];\n return {best:cands[0], all:cands, confirm:true};\n }\n\n // SS400 一般板：依種類查詢，並過濾各廠有無此板厚\n const tblMap = {square:PLATE_PRICES.square, profile:PLATE_PRICES.profile, circle:PLATE_PRICES.circle};\n const tbl = tblMap[pt] || PLATE_PRICES.square;\n const row = tbl.find(r => thickness <= r.max);\n if(!row) return null;\n const candidates = [\n hasFuk && row.fukgen ? {name:\'福源\', px:row.fukgen} : null,\n hasGuan && row.guan ? {name:\'冠華\', px:row.guan} : null,\n ].filter(Boolean);\n if(!candidates.length) return null;\n return {best:candidates.reduce((a,b)=>a.px>b.px?a:b), all:candidates};\n}\n\n// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n// 規格警示\n// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\nfunction checkSpec(shape, dims, matCode) {\n const tol = 0.05;\n const near = (arr, v) => arr && arr.some(x => Math.abs(Number(x)-v) <= tol);\n if(shape===\'pipe\') {\n const pipeMat = document.getElementById(\'pipe-mat-sel\')?.value || \'stkm\';\n if(pipeMat===\'sus\') {\n const ok = SUS_PIPE_PRICES.some(r => Math.abs(r.od-dims.OD)<=0.1 && Math.abs(r.id-dims.ID)<=0.1);\n return {ok, msg: ok ? \'Ø\'+dims.OD+\'×Ø\'+dims.ID+\' ✓ 現貨規格（洋田）\' : \'⚠ Ø\'+dims.OD+\'×Ø\'+dims.ID+\' 洋田無此規格\'};\n } else if(pipeMat===\'al\') {\n const ok = AL_PIPE_PRICES.some(r => Math.abs(r.od-dims.OD)<=0.1 && Math.abs(r.id-dims.ID)<=0.1);\n return {ok, msg: ok ? \'Ø\'+dims.OD+\'×Ø\'+dims.ID+\' ✓ 現貨規格（洋田）\' : \'⚠ Ø\'+dims.OD+\'×Ø\'+dims.ID+\' 洋田無此規格\'};\n } else {\n // STKM13A：從下拉選的就是建興現貨，直接顯示確認\n return {ok: true, msg: \'Ø\'+dims.OD+\'×Ø\'+dims.ID+\' ✓ 現貨規格（建興）\'};\n }\n }\n const arr = getSpecArr(matCode, shape);\n if(!arr || arr.length===0) return {ok:true, msg:\'\'};\n if(shape===\'plate\') {\n const ok=near(arr,dims.T);\n return {ok, msg:ok?\'板厚 \'+dims.T+\'mm ✓ 現貨規格\':\'⚠ 板厚 \'+dims.T+\'mm 非現貨規格，請確認採購\'};\n }\n if(shape===\'bar_rnd\') {\n const ok=near(arr,dims.D);\n return {ok, msg:ok?\'Ø\'+dims.D+\'mm ✓ 現貨規格（\'+matCode+\'）\':\'⚠ Ø\'+dims.D+\'mm 非現貨規格（\'+matCode+\'），請確認採購\'};\n }\n if(shape===\'bar_sq\') {\n const ok=near(arr,dims.A);\n return {ok, msg:ok?\'□\'+dims.A+\'mm ✓ 現貨規格（\'+matCode+\'）\':\'⚠ □\'+dims.A+\'mm 非現貨規格（\'+matCode+\'），請確認採購\'};\n }\n if(shape===\'bar_hex\') {\n const ok=near(arr,dims.S);\n return {ok, msg:ok?\'⬡\'+dims.S+\'mm ✓ 現貨規格（\'+matCode+\'）\':\'⚠ ⬡\'+dims.S+\'mm 非現貨規格（\'+matCode+\'），請確認採購\'};\n }\n return {ok:true, msg:\'\'};\n}\n\n// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n// 材質切換\n// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n// ── parseFloat(document.getElementById(\'dim-FW\')?.value) || 0;\n const box = document.getElementById(\'allowance-display\');\n if(!fT || !fL || !fW) {\n if(box) box.innerHTML = \'輸入完成尺寸後自動計算採購規格 →\';\n [\'dim-T\',\'dim-L\',\'dim-W\'].forEach(id => { const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value=\'0\'; });\n return;\n }\n // 預留量規則（依完成T判斷）：T ≤ 100 → 單邊2.5；T > 100 → 單邊5\n const allow = fT <= 100 ? 2.5 : 5;\n const buyL = Math.round((fL + allow * 2) * 10) / 10;\n const buyW = Math.round((fW + allow * 2) * 10) / 10;\n // 板厚：直接用完成T查現貨，找「> 完成T且最接近」的板厚（不加預留）\n const specArr = nearest;\n specNote = \'完成厚 \'+fT+\'mm → 取現貨 \'+buyT+\'mm\';\n } else {\n buyT = Math.ceil(fT + 1);\n specNote = \'完成厚 \'+fT+\'mm，超出現貨範圍，取 \'+buyT+\'mm\';\n }\n }\n // 寫入隱藏欄位供 calc 使用\n const hidT = \'+\n \'
\'+\n \'T\'+\n \'\'+buyT+\'\'+\n \'×\'+\n \'L\'+\n \'\'+buyL+\'\'+\n \'×\'+\n \'W\'+\n \'\'+buyW+\'\'+\n \'mm\'+\n \'

LW單邊預留 \'+allow+\'mm\u3000｜\u3000\'+specNote+\'

sizeLabel=\'≤299mm → ×1.0\'; }\n else if(maxDim <= 499) { sizeCoeff=1.5; sizeLabel=\'300~499mm → ×1.5\'; }\n else if(maxDim <= 699) { sizeCoeff=1.8; sizeLabel=\'500~699mm → ×1.8\'; }\n else {\n document.getElementById(\'milling-result\').innerHTML =\n \'
⚠ 尺寸 ≥700mm，須另行報價

\n
材質係數${mc.label}
${chamferRow}\n
\n
`;\n}\n\nfunction buildMillingCard(matName, finL, finW, finT) {\n const showChamfer = document.getElementById(\'milling-type\')?.value !== \'six\';\n const chamferHTML = showChamfer ? `\n

vol = 0, formula = \'\';\n if(shape===\'plate\') {\n vol = dims.L * dims.W * dims.T;\n formula = dims.L+\' × \'+dims.W+\' × \'+dims.T+\' × \'+density+\' ÷ 1,000,000\';\n } else if(shape===\'bar_rnd\') return; }\n\n const matVal = document.getElementById(\'mat\').value.split(\'|\');\n const matName = matVal[0];\n let density = parseFloat(matVal[1]);\n const g = id => parseFloat(document.getElementById(id)?.value) || 0;\n\n let specDesc = \'\', impliedPx = 0, dbPrices = [], sawFee = 0, unit = \'元/kg\', weight = 0;\n\n // ── 成品管 ──\n if(currentShape===\'prod_pipe\') {\n const si = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'dim-PP_SPEC\')?.value||\'0\');\n const tp = document.getElementById(\'dim-PP_TYPE\')?.value || \'fin\';\n const L = g(\'dim-L\');\n if(!L) { alert(\'請填入長度\'); return; }\n const p = STKM_PRICES[si];\n const typeLabel = {fin:\'成品管\',raw:\'素管\',plt:\'電鍍管\'};\n const keyMap = {\n fin:[{name:\'益陽\',k:\'yi_fin\'},{name:\'東祐\',k:\'dy_fin\'},{name:\'金亞洲\',k:\'jy_fin\'}],\n raw:[{name:\'益陽\',k:\'yi_raw\'},{name:\'東祐\',k:\'dy_raw\'}],\n plt:[{name:\'益陽\',k:\'yi_plt\'},{name:\'金亞洲\',k:\'jy_plt\'}]\n };\n dbPrices = keyMap[tp].map(x=>({name:x.name,px:p[x.k]})).filter(x=>x.px);\n impliedPx = purchaseAmt / L;\n unit = \'元/mm\';\n specDesc = \'STKM13C \'+typeLabel[tp]+\' Ø\'+p.id+\'×Ø\'+p.od+\' × \'+L+\' mm\';\n }\n // ── 硬鉻軸心 ──\n else if(currentShape===\'shaft\') {\n const idx = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'dim-SH_DIA\').value);\n const mat = document.getElementById(\'dim-SH_MAT\').value;\n const L = g(\'dim-L\');\n if(!L) { alert(\'請填入長度\'); return; }\n const s = SHAFT_PRICES[idx];\n const matMap = {s45c:\'S45C硬鉻\',scm:\'SCM440+QT\',hf:\'高週波\',sus:\'SUS304\'};\n const pxMap = {\n s45c:[{name:\'金亞洲\',px:s.jy_s45c},{name:\'益陽\',px:s.yi_s45c}],\n scm :[{name:\'金亞洲\',px:s.jy_scm}],\n hf :[{name:\'金亞洲\',px:s.jy_hf}],\n sus :[{name:\'金亞洲\',px:s.jy_sus},{name:\'益陽\',px:s.yi_sus}]\n };\n dbPrices = (pxMap[mat]||[]).filter(x=>x.px);\n sawFee = L <= 200 ? 15 : 0;\n impliedPx = (purchaseAmt - sawFee) / L;\n unit = \'元/mm\';\n specDesc = \'硬鉻軸心 \'+matMap[mat]+\' Ø\'+s.dia+\' × \'+L+\' mm\';\n }\n // ── 管料 ──\n else if(currentShape===\'pipe\') {\n const pipeMat = document.getElementById(\'pipe-mat-sel\')?.value || \'stkm\';\n let OD, ID, L;\n if(pipeMat===\'sus\') {\n const idx = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'dim-SUS_SPEC\')?.value||\'0\');\n const p = SUS_PIPE_PRICES[idx];\n OD=p.od; ID=p.id; L=g(\'dim-L-sus\');\n dbPrices = [{name:\'洋田整支\',px:p.whole},{name:\'洋田定尺\',px:p.cut}].filter(x=>x.px);\n } else if(pipeMat===\'al\') {\n const idx = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'dim-AL_SPEC\')?.value||\'0\');\n const p = AL_PIPE_PRICES[idx];\n OD=p.od; ID=p.id; L=g(\'dim-L-al\');\n dbPrices = [{name:\'洋田整支\',px:p.whole},{name:\'洋田定尺\',px:p.cut}].filter(x=>x.px);\n } else {\n // STKM13A：重量計價（元/kg）\n const si = parseInt(document.getElementById(\'dim-STKM_SPEC\')?.value||\'0\');\n const [od_,id__] = PIPE_SPEC[si];\n OD=od_; ID=id__; L=parseFloat(document.getElementById(\'dim-L-steel\')?.value)||0;\n // STKM13A 無元/kg行情可比，dbPrices 留空\n }\n if(!OD || !ID || !L) { alert(\'請填入所有尺寸\'); return; }\n sawFee = Math.round(OD / 10 * 10) / 10;\n\n if(pipeMat===\'stkm\') {\n // ── STKM13A 重量計價 ──\n const {kg:k} = calcWeight(\'pipe\', {OD, ID, L}, 7.85);\n weight = k;\n impliedPx = weight > 0 ? (purchaseAmt - sawFee) / weight : 0;\n unit = \'元/kg\';\n } else {\n // ── SUS / AL 元/mm 計價 ──\n impliedPx = (purchaseAmt - sawFee) / L;\n unit = \'元/mm\';\n }\n const pmMap = {stkm:\'STKM13A\', sus:\'SUS304管\', al:\'鋁管\'};\n specDesc = pmMap[pipeMat]+\' Ø\'+ID+\'×Ø\'+OD+\' × \'+L+\' mm\';\n }\n // ── 板材 + 棒材 ──\n else {\n let dims={}, valid=true;\n if(currentShape===\'plate\') {\n dims={L:g(\'dim-L\'),W:g(\'dim-W\'),T:g(\'dim-T\')};\n if(!dims.L||!dims.W||!dims.T) valid=false;\n } else if(currentShape===\'bar_rnd\') {\n const idVal=parseFloat(document.getElementById(\'dim-ID\')?.value)||0;\n dims={D:g(\'dim-D\'),L:g(\'dim-L\'),...(idVal>0?{ID:idVal}:{})};\n if(!dims.D||!dims.L) valid=false;\n } else if(currentShape===\'bar_sq\') {\n dims={A:g(\'dim-A\'),L:g(\'dim-L\')};\n if(!dims.A||!dims.L) valid=false;\n } else if(currentShape===\'bar_hex\') {\n dims={S:g(\'dim-S\'),L:g(\'dim-L\')};\n if(!dims.S||!dims.L) valid=false;\n }\n if(!valid) { alert(\'請填入所有尺寸\'); return; }\n if(isNaN(density)) density=7.85;\n\n const copperBarMats=[\'C3604\',\'BC6\',\'PBC3\',\'ALBC3\',\'HBrass\'];\n const isCopperBar = copperBarMats.includes(matName) && currentShape===\'bar_rnd\';\n const isFCD45 = matName===\'FCD45\';\n\n // 計算重量（反查模式一律實寸，銅棒用實際密度，不乘1.03）\n if(isCopperBar) {\n const {kg:k} = calcWeight(currentShape, dims, density);\n weight = k;\n } else {\n const {kg:k}=calcWeight(currentShape,dims,density);\n weight=currentShape===\'plate\'&&k<1?1:k;\n }\n\n // 切工費（取各廠最高）\n if([\'bar_rnd\',\'bar_sq\',\'bar_hex\'].includes(currentShape) && !isCopperBar && !isFCD45) {\n const dim = currentShape===\'bar_rnd\'?dims.D:currentShape===\'bar_sq\'?dims.A:dims.S;\n const res = lookupBarPrice(matName, currentShape, dim);\n if(res) {\n sawFee = res.all.reduce((mx,v)=>Math.max(mx, getVendorSawFee(v.name,matName,currentShape,dim,dims.L)), 0);\n dbPrices = res.all.filter(v=>v.px);\n }\n } else if(isCopperBar) {\n const cpx = matName===\'PBC3\'?600:matName===\'C3604\'?240:matName===\'ALBC3\'?660:650;\n dbPrices=[{name:\'行情參考（實寸）\',px:cpx}];\n } else if(currentShape===\'plate\') {\n const plateType=document.getElementById(\'plate-type-sel\')?.value||\'square\';\n const res=lookupPlatePrice(dims.T,matName,plateType);\n if(res&&!res.inquiry) dbPrices=res.all.filter(v=>v.px);\n }\n\n impliedPx = weight>0 ? (purchaseAmt-sawFee)/weight : 0;\n\n // 規格描述\n const matDisp = matName.replace(\'S45C_L\',\'S45C 光中碳\').replace(\'S45C_B\',\'S45C 黑中碳\');\n if(currentShape===\'plate\') specDesc=matDisp+\' 板材\u3000T\'+dims.T+\' × L\'+dims.L+\' × W\'+dims.W+\' mm\';\n else if(currentShape===\'bar_rnd\') specDesc=matDisp+\' 圓棒\u3000Ø\'+dims.D+\' × \'+dims.L+\' mm\';\n else if(currentShape===\'bar_sq\') specDesc=matDisp+\' 四角棒\u3000□\'+dims.A+\' × \'+dims.L+\' mm\';\n else if(currentShape===\'bar_hex\') specDesc=matDisp+\' 六角棒\u3000⬡\'+dims.S+\' × \'+dims.L+\' mm\';\n }\n\n // 比較列表\n let compRows = \'\';\n if(dbPrices.length>0) {\n compRows = dbPrices.map(v=>{\n if(!v.px) return \'\';\n const diff = (impliedPx - v.px) / v.px * 100;\n const absDiff = Math.abs(diff);\n let cls=\'diff-ok\', mark=\'✓ 合理\';\n if(absDiff>10){cls=\'diff-alert\'; mark=diff>0?\'⚠ 偏高\':\'⚠ 偏低\';}\n else if(absDiff>5){cls=\'diff-warn\'; mark=diff>0?\'△ 略高\':\'△ 略低\';}\n const sign=diff>=0?\'+\':\'\';\n return `
\n ${v.name}\u3000${fmtPx(v.px)} ${unit}\n ${sign}${diff.toFixed(1)}%\u3000${mark}\n
`;\n }).join(\'\');\n } else {\n compRows=\'
此規格無內建資料庫行情，無法比較
\';\n }\n\n const sawNote = sawFee>0 ? `
扣除切工費− NT$ ${fmtNT(sawFee)}
` : \'\';\n const wgtNote = weight>0 ? `
計算重量${fmt(weight)} kg
` : \'\';\n\n document.getElementById(\'result\').innerHTML = `\n
\n
反查結果 ／ ${specDesc}
\n
\n
\n
IMPLIED UNIT PRICE
\n
${fmtPx(impliedPx)} ${unit}
\n
進料 NT$ ${fmtNT(purchaseAmt)}${sawFee>0?\' ／ 切工 NT$\'+fmtNT(sawFee)+\' 已扣除\':\'\'}
\n
\n
\n ${wgtNote}\n ${sawNote}\n
進料金額NT$ ${fmtNT(purchaseAmt)}
\n
\n
\n
\n
\n
資料庫行情比較
\n
\n
\n ✓ 合理 ±5%\u3000△ 注意 5–10%\u3000⚠ 差異過大 >10%\n
\n ${compRows}\n
\n
`;\n}\n\n'; document.head.appendChild(matScript); })();
